- **REQ-UI-051** — User-visible English left in `@code` blocks on localized pages (BRD-91, added 2026-08-01).
  - *Problem:* the coverage counter measures MARKUP by design, so user-visible strings built in `@code` are outside its measured population and no test catches them. Observed live in Hindi: `Local socket`, `Disconnected` on `/qdrant-admin`.
  - *Acceptance:* the known members are de-staticked and localized; the counter is extended to measure `@code` literals that reach the user (or a separate test is added); bound `Select` VALUES stay culture-invariant while their display text localizes.
<a id="d-req-ui-052"></a>
- **REQ-UI-052** — Native macOS menu bar and auth screens are hardcoded English (BRD-91, added 2026-08-01).
  - *Acceptance:* `MainPage.BuildMenuBar` (File/Go/View/Help) and `Login.razor`/`Register.razor` localized in `en`+`hi` and added to the `LocalizedFiles` registry.
  - ⚠ *Coupling:* `tests/appium/nav.py` `go_menu()` matches the menu by English text and is the chromeless-recovery path — it must move to resource keys in the SAME change or the harness breaks.
<a id="d-req-ui-057"></a>
- **REQ-UI-057** — `ConnectorEndToEndTests` port-binding flake (added 2026-08-02). ⚠ Test-infrastructure defect, not a product defect.
  - *Problem:* `HttpListenerException: Address already in use` thrown from the fixture's `Dispose`. Independently reported by three clusters and reproduced once by the orchestrator; passes on re-run and in isolation (77/77).
  - *Cost:* it makes every full-suite run non-deterministic, which trains people to re-run rather than investigate — the habit that hides a real failure.
  - *Acceptance:* the fixture binds an ephemeral port and releases it deterministically; 20 consecutive full-suite runs with zero occurrences.
<a id="d-req-ui-056"></a>
- **REQ-UI-056** — Unblock the deferred connector/scheduling strings and decide the persisted-English policy (BRD-91, added 2026-08-02).
  - *Problem:* 65 connector literals could not be converted because `Automations.razor` renders `ScheduleRunItem.Reason`, `ScheduleRun.FailureReason` and `ScheduleRun.Detail` RAW for every job kind — emitting keys would print key names on screen.
  - *Unblock (one line):* bind the Automations schedule column to a live `CronDescriber.Describe(CronExpression.Parse(row.CronExpression), Localize)` and keep the stored `ScheduleText` purely as the confirmation audit record. The API is already shaped for it.
  - ⚠ *Policy decision required:* those three columns are **persisted English rows**. Rows already written cannot be re-rendered, so any conversion needs a permanent key-or-legacy-text fallback. Decide it deliberately rather than discovering it mid-conversion.
  - *Also in scope:* `DatabaseMaintenanceJobHandler` (7 literals) and `JobRunner` (3), both blocked on the same `IScheduledJobHandler` string contract, which `ConnectorJobHandler` also implements.
<a id="d-req-ui-055"></a>
- **REQ-UI-055** — Service-layer English backlog: 569 prose literals across 88 files (BRD-91, added 2026-08-02).
  - *Problem:* user-visible English is built in `apps/TechieDesk.Core/Services/`, outside every coverage counter the project has. REQ-UI-051 fixed the six known sites and measured the rest.
  - *Frozen, not fixed:* `ServiceStringCoverage.TheServiceLayerNeverGrowsMoreEnglish` ratchets the count at 569 so it cannot grow.
  - *Acceptance:* the confirmed-rendered clusters localized in priority order (connectors → scheduling → licensing → backup/Docker → leaf files → agent skill results), the ratchet lowered as each lands, the wire vocabulary provably unchanged at each step, and machine-facing prose classified via `MachineText` rather than translated.
  - ⚠ The 569 counts machine-facing prose too — the deliverable is the user-visible subset, not the number.
<a id="d-req-ui-054"></a>
- **REQ-UI-054** — The `View` menu is declared but never reaches the macOS menu bar (BRD-133, added 2026-08-01).
  - *Problem:* `MainPage.xaml.cs:149-165` declares a `MenuView` `MenuBarItem` with Zoom In / Zoom Out / Actual Size and adds it to `MenuBarItems`, but the running app's accessibility tree contains none of those items in either language. Three declared accelerators (⌘+, ⌘−, ⌘0) do not exist for the user.
  - ⚠ *Pre-existing, not a localization regression* — the English strings are equally absent.
  - *Acceptance:* the zoom items appear in the running menu bar in `en` and `hi` with their accelerators operable, **verified on the live head** — a source scan cannot close this row.
<a id="d-req-ui-053"></a>
- **REQ-UI-053** — Sidebar nav links carry no `AutomationId` (added 2026-08-01). ⚠ UI-testability coding-standard defect.
  - *Problem:* measured on the live app — every sidebar `XCUIElementTypeLink` has `identifier=""` and the `href` is not surfaced in the accessibility tree, so the verify harness can only locate links by localized display text.
  - *Acceptance:* all 22 `MainLayout.razor` `SidebarMenuButton` entries carry a stable route-derived `AutomationId` surfaced as `identifier`; `tests/appium/nav.py` selects every entry by identifier alone with no resource lookup.

## Functional requirements

<a id="d-req-fn-001"></a>
- **REQ-FN-001** — RSA-OAEP-SHA256 encryption of every password field; public key fetched from `GET /AuthSvc/public-key` and cached; DECRYPTION_FAILED → refetch key, retry once; no plaintext password ever transmitted/stored (BRD-14).
<a id="d-req-fn-002"></a>
- **REQ-FN-002** — Silent access-token refresh via `POST /AuthSvc/refresh` before expiry; refresh failure → login redirect preserving route; tokens held server-side (BRD-15).
<a id="d-req-fn-003"></a>
- **REQ-FN-003** — All product routes require auth; unauthenticated → `/login` with deep-link return (BRD-20).
<a id="d-req-fn-004"></a>
- **REQ-FN-004** — Single `AppManagerClient` sends `X-Api-Key`/`X-Api-Secret` on every call; credentials from env/user-secrets (BRD-21). Isolates the v1.4 wire contract (a-prefixed URL params).
<a id="d-req-fn-005"></a>
- **REQ-FN-005** — Map app-scoped `applicationRole` → Admin/Manager/User per BRD §5 (BRD-23).
<a id="d-req-fn-006"></a>
- **REQ-FN-006** — Enforce the role capability matrix (Admin: instance + all; Manager: workspace/doc/connector mgmt; User: assigned workspaces + own data) (BRD-24).
<a id="d-req-fn-007"></a>
- **REQ-FN-007** — Every authorization check server-side; UI hiding never sufficient (BRD-25).
  - *Acceptance:* forging a request to a role-gated operation as User returns denied.
<a id="d-req-fn-008"></a>
- **REQ-FN-008** — User↔workspace assignment stored in App DB; User sees only assigned (BRD-29).
<a id="d-req-fn-009"></a>
- **REQ-FN-009** — Default workspace auto-created on first run (BRD-31).
<a id="d-req-fn-010"></a>
- **REQ-FN-010** — Export thread as Markdown or JSON (BRD-35).
<a id="d-req-fn-011"></a>
- **REQ-FN-011** — Permanently delete a thread or entire own history (BRD-36).
<a id="d-req-fn-012"></a>
- **REQ-FN-012** — Delete document removes vectors from all using workspaces, with confirmation (BRD-45).
<a id="d-req-fn-013"></a>
- **REQ-FN-013** — License validated at login + periodically via `POST /LicenseSvc/validate`; name/status/expiry shown (BRD-49).
<a id="d-req-fn-014"></a>
- **REQ-FN-014** — Feature gating via FeatureSvc (binary + level); gated features show upgrade prompt (BRD-50).
<a id="d-req-fn-015"></a>
- **REQ-FN-015** — AppManager unreachable → last-known-good license honored for configurable grace period; banner shown (BRD-51).
<a id="d-req-fn-016"></a>
- **REQ-FN-016** — Wizard detects local Ollama and offers discovered models (BRD-53).
<a id="d-req-fn-017"></a>
- **REQ-FN-017** — Dockerfile + compose: app + Postgres/pgvector, optional Qdrant/Ollama profiles; one-command boot incl. TechieDeskDb migration on start (BRD-55).
<a id="d-req-fn-018"></a>
- **REQ-FN-018** — All deployment config via environment variables (BRD-56).
<a id="d-req-fn-019"></a>
- **REQ-FN-019** — Named volumes for App DB, uploads, ONNX model — upgrade-safe (BRD-57).
<a id="d-req-fn-020"></a>
- **REQ-FN-020** — Connector runs as background jobs: progress, per-item results + failure reasons (BRD-65).
<a id="d-req-fn-021"></a>
- **REQ-FN-021** — REST API for workspaces/documents/threads/chat incl. streaming (SSE) (BRD-66).
<a id="d-req-fn-022"></a>
- **REQ-FN-022** — Admin API-key management (create/label/revoke); key auth on every API call; keys stored hashed (BRD-67, BRD-95).
<a id="d-req-fn-023"></a>
- **REQ-FN-023** — Swagger/OpenAPI UI at `/api/docs` (BRD-68).
<a id="d-req-fn-024"></a>
- **REQ-FN-024** — API usage metered; `API_REQUESTS` license level enforced when licensing active (BRD-69).
<a id="d-req-fn-025"></a>
- **REQ-FN-025** — Widget script served by instance; workspace-scoped; API-key-authenticated; license-gated `EMBED_WIDGET` (BRD-70).
<a id="d-req-fn-026"></a>
- **REQ-FN-026** — Promo-code validation via `POST /PaymentSvc/promo-codes/validate` (BRD-79).
<a id="d-req-fn-027"></a>
- **REQ-FN-027** — Close own support issue (`POST /IssueSvc/{id}/close`); ALREADY_CLOSED handled (BRD-82).
<a id="d-req-fn-028"></a>
- **REQ-FN-028** — Cron scheduling for agent/ingestion jobs with run history (BRD-93).
<a id="d-req-fn-029"></a>
- **REQ-FN-029** — All app data access via Dapper: parameterized SQL (SQLite) / stored procedures or queries (PostgreSQL + pgvector); zero EF Core references (BRD-102).
  - *Acceptance:* `grep` of all TechieDesk csproj/code finds no EntityFrameworkCore reference.
<a id="d-req-fn-030"></a>
- **REQ-FN-030** — TechieDeskDb console project applies versioned idempotent DbUp migrations (journaled, ordered); runs standalone + at container start; non-zero exit blocks app start; outcomes via Serilog (BRD-103).
<a id="d-req-fn-031"></a>
- **REQ-FN-031** — Migration scripts per provider (SQLite + PostgreSQL) produce equivalent schemas; verified by booting the app against each (BRD-104).
<a id="d-req-fn-032"></a>
- **REQ-FN-032** — ⚠ **BLOCKER (new 2026-07-20).** Authenticated session must survive the post-login navigation so AppManager mode is actually usable.
  - *Root cause:* `Login.razor:117` `Nav.NavigateTo(SafeReturnUrl(), forceLoad: true)` destroys the Blazor circuit; `SessionTokenStore` is per-circuit by design, so the replacement circuit has no session and `IRouteGuard` redirects to `/login` — indefinitely.
  - *Acceptance:* a user who authenticates successfully against AppManager lands on the requested route in an authenticated session, and a page refresh does not sign them out.
  - *Design tension (owner decision):* the fix needs session continuity across circuits — an auth cookie or ASP.NET Core protected browser storage — which weakens the "tokens are never written to browser storage, cookies, or exposed to JavaScript" property that REQ-FN-002 and REQ-NFR-004 were built around. A signed, HttpOnly, Secure, SameSite cookie carrying only a session handle (not the JWTs) is the usual reconciliation, keeping the tokens themselves server-side. **Not chosen unilaterally.**
  - *Blast radius:* blocks live UAT for REQ-UI-006…013 and REQ-FN-002/003/013/014/015 — i.e. the entire ~27-row "pending owner UAT" track.
  - *2026-07-25 RESOLVED* — owner chose the signed-cookie/opaque-handle reconciliation; see the Requirements Status row.
<a id="d-req-fn-034"></a>
- **REQ-FN-034** — ⚠ **BLOCKER (new 2026-07-25).** The DbUp migrator and the running app must resolve the SAME application database.
  - *Root cause:* `apps/TechieDeskDb/MigrationRunner.cs` — `DefaultSqliteConnectionString = "Data Source=data/techiedesk.db"`, resolved relative to the **current working directory**. `apps/TechieDesk/Services/Data/AppDbConnectionFactory.cs` — defaults to `Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "data", "techiedesk.db")`, resolved relative to **bin/**. A plain `dotnet run` from the project directory makes these two different files.
  - *Observed 2026-07-25:* `apps/TechieDesk/data/techiedesk.db` = 61,440 bytes (migrated) vs `apps/TechieDesk/bin/Debug/net10.0/data/techiedesk.db` = **0 bytes** (what the app opens). `/workspace/default/settings` renders "⚠️ Workspace not available"; server log `SQLite Error 1: 'no such table: WorkspaceAssignment'` from `WorkspaceAssignmentRepository.cs:58`. The boot log still says "Database migrations applied successfully".
  - *Why it hid for so long:* the workspace **list** in the sidebar is served by the separate TechieRag store (`techiedesk-rag-store.db`), which is healthy — so the app looks fine until an app-DB-backed screen is opened. Any launch where CWD and BaseDirectory coincide (or where `AppDb__ConnectionString` is set explicitly) masks it entirely, which is how REQ-FN-008/030 and REQ-UI-015 were legitimately Verified on 2026-07-18.
  - *Acceptance:* a fresh `dotnet run` with no `AppDb` configuration produces an app that reads the database DbUp just migrated; `/workspace/{slug}/settings` loads all four tabs; the regression test in `tests/verify/build-2026-07-25-smoke.spec.ts` passes.
  - *Owner decision needed:* which path is canonical. It is not a free choice — under Docker both CWD and `AppContext.BaseDirectory` resolve under `/app`, and REQ-FN-019 requires the file to land on the volume-backed `data/` mount. Deliberately not chosen unilaterally.
  - *Blast radius:* REQ-UI-015, REQ-FN-008, REQ-FN-030 demoted from Verified; blocks live verification of the REQ-RAG-047 workspace rerank toggle (that toggle lives on the broken screen).

<a id="d-req-fn-035"></a>
- **REQ-FN-035** — Convert `apps/TechieDesk` to a .NET 10 **MAUI Blazor Hybrid** host for macOS (Mac Catalyst) and Windows (BRD-128, added 2026-07-26).
  - *Acceptance:* the app builds and launches as a native desktop application on both platforms, rendering the existing Razor components in a `BlazorWebView`; no Kestrel, no `localhost` URL, no container; every shipped screen reachable from the native window.
  - *Scope note:* the Razor components, TrBlazeUI usage, and the TechieRag integration are expected to carry over unchanged. What changes is the host: the `Program.cs` web pipeline, DI lifetimes (scoped-per-circuit becomes scoped-per-app), and every code path that assumed an `HttpContext`, a circuit, or an HTTP request.
  - *Depends on:* nothing; this is the root of the desktop track. REQ-FN-036/039/041 sequence behind it.

<a id="d-req-fn-036"></a>
- **REQ-FN-036** — The app must be fully usable with **no account** (BRD-129, added 2026-07-26).
  - *Acceptance:* a first launch with no AppManager configuration reaches a working workspace and can ingest a document and get a cited answer without any sign-in; `/login` is reachable from settings and never redirected to at launch.
  - *Inverts:* the standing assumption that offline mode is an evaluation-only fallback (BRD §12).

<a id="d-req-fn-037"></a>
- **REQ-FN-037** — All persistent state lives in the **per-user OS data directory**, with one-time migration (BRD-130, added 2026-07-26).
  - *Acceptance:* on macOS every artefact resolves under `~/Library/Application Support/TechieDesk`, on Windows under `%LOCALAPPDATA%\TechieDesk`; an install carrying the old app-relative `data/` directory has it relocated once, losing nothing; DbUp runs in-process at launch against that same directory.
  - *Relationship to REQ-FN-034:* this extends it, it does not replace it. `DataDirectory` remains the single authority and `RelocateLegacyArtefact` already implements the move; only the resolved root changes. Running the migration in-process closes the FN-034 defect class structurally — one process, one resolution, no second path to disagree with.

<a id="d-req-fn-038"></a>
- **REQ-FN-038** — Signed, installable packages per platform + an update check (BRD-131, added 2026-07-26).
  - *Acceptance:* a notarized macOS `.app`/DMG and a signed Windows MSIX/installer each install and launch on a clean machine without security warnings; updating preserves the data directory.
  - **⚠ SCOPE CORRECTED 2026-07-27 — owner challenge, and the correction is upheld.** This row previously read *"an external dependency, not a code task"*, which conflated the signing **identity** (owner-supplied) with the packaging **pipeline** (code). The consequence was real, not cosmetic: the row was treated as owner-blocked, no pipeline work was scheduled, and the build phase was allowed to look complete while **no CI job builds the desktop app at all** — `.github/workflows/` contains exactly one workflow, `publish-nuget.yml`, and it packs only `src/TechieRag` + `src/TechieRag.Embedded` on `ubuntu-latest`. There is today no path by which anyone, owner included, obtains an installable TechieDesk artefact. Now split so the code half cannot hide behind the owner half:
    - **REQ-FN-038a — CI/CD build + package workflow. Buildable now, blocks on nothing.** A `.github/workflows/publish-desktop.yml` mirroring the NuGet workflow's shape (tag-triggered + `workflow_dispatch`, version derived from the tag, blocking test gate, artefact upload, GitHub Release attachment). Must use a **`macos-latest` runner** for `-f net10.0-maccatalyst` → `.app`/DMG and a **`windows-latest` runner** for `-f net10.0-windows10.0.19041.0` → MSIX; `ubuntu-latest` cannot build either head. This is also the only route by which the **Windows head is ever compiled** — see REQ-FN-035 PARTIAL, which is unfixable from this macOS host and would be closed structurally by a Windows runner.
    - **REQ-FN-038b — in-app update check. IMPLEMENTED 2026-07-27.** Reads the published version feed, offers the update, and preserves `~/Library/Application Support/TechieDesk` / `%LOCALAPPDATA%\TechieDesk` across versions (REQ-FN-037 owns the directory; this must never write inside the bundle — the same defect class already found and fixed there for Serilog).
      - *Scope actually delivered:* check, channel policy (stable/prerelease), release notes, per-platform asset selection, download into the data directory, and an opt-in automatic check at launch. **Install is not implemented and that is a decision, not a gap** — see below.
      - *Where the UI lives:* `docs/TechieDesk-UIDesign.md` places the Updates card as a tab of the **Phase 3–4** App settings screen (`/admin/settings`), which does not exist. Rather than build a Phase 3–4 screen to host it, the card lives at its own route `/settings/updates` — exactly the precedent REQ-UI-041 set with `/settings/data` — and lifts into that tab unchanged when the screen is built. All four elements the design specifies are present (`Card` + `Switch` ×3 + `Button` ×2 + `Alert`).
      - ⚠ *Why "install in the background" is rendered DISABLED rather than working or omitted:* the packages published today are unsigned (038c), so a download cannot be cryptographically attributed to the publisher. An updater that silently replaced the running binary with an unverifiable download would be a remote-code-execution path presented as a feature — one spoofed or compromised feed response and it executes attacker-supplied code with the user's privileges, automatically, at launch. Downloading over TLS and handing the file to the operator keeps Gatekeeper/SmartScreen in the loop. This becomes defensible the moment 038c signs the artefacts and the signature can be verified before anything runs.
      - ⚠ *Egress policy — the automatic launch check defaults to OFF.* The pre-existing `OutboundEgressTests` structural guard failed the build when the two new `HttpClient` consumers appeared, which is precisely why it exists. REQ-NFR-008 / BRD-99 state that nothing leaves the instance except calls to operator-configured providers and to AppManager; an update check contacts a host the operator never configured. So it is opt-in, sends no credential, and is claimed once per launch (`UpdateLaunchState`) so navigation cannot turn it into polling. The cost is stated plainly: with it off, a published security fix is not announced until someone looks. Reversing that default is one line but belongs with a decision on REQ-NFR-008.
    - **REQ-FN-038c — sign + notarize. Genuinely owner-blocked.** Apple Developer ID + Windows code-signing certificate, supplied as repository secrets. Model it on `publish-nuget.yml`'s existing precedent: that workflow maps `NUGET_API_KEY` into job env and gates the push step on `env.NUGET_API_KEY != ''`, publishing nothing rather than failing when the secret is absent. The desktop workflow should likewise **build and upload unsigned artefacts when no identity is present**, so 038a is verifiable and useful before 038c exists.
  - *Dependency the split makes visible:* **REQ-FN-039 cannot be proven live until 038c lands** — Mac Catalyst refuses Keychain to an unsigned build (`errSecMissingEntitlement`), and an ad-hoc signature cannot carry `keychain-access-groups`. Keeping 038 parked therefore also kept FN-039 permanently at 75%.
  - **2026-07-27 [REQ-FN-038a] — Implemented (40% of the parent row), self-smoked end-to-end.**
    - *Files:* `.github/workflows/publish-desktop.yml` (new), `.github/packaging/macos-INSTALL.txt` (new), `.github/packaging/windows-INSTALL.txt` (new), `apps/TechieDesk/TechieDesk.csproj` (the `MtouchInterpreter` fix below).
    - *Jobs:* `version` (one authoritative version; a numeric-only `display_version` is derived separately because `CFBundleShortVersionString` rejects a prerelease suffix) → `test` (blocking, `ubuntu-latest`) → `macos` + `windows` in parallel → `release` (tag only). Triggers on `desktop-v*` tags and `workflow_dispatch`, deliberately **not** the `v*` tags `publish-nuget.yml` uses: the library and the app version independently, and a library release should not spend macOS/Windows runner minutes.
    - *What was actually proven here, not merely built:* the Catalyst head was published in **Release** for the first time (it had only ever been built in Debug), a DMG was produced with `hdiutil`, **mounted, the app copied out of it, and that installed copy launched and rendered the workspace shell**. Universal binary confirmed by `file` (x86_64 + arm64), ad-hoc signature confirmed intact after the round-trip by `codesign -v`. 417 MB bundle compresses to a 73 MB DMG.
    - ⚠ **A crash that only Release exposes, and the reason the smoke gate in this workflow is a hard gate.** The first Release `.app` passed *every* structural check — correct Info.plist, both architectures, valid signature — and aborted with SIGABRT the moment it launched, before writing a single log line: `Failed to load AOT module 'Docker.DotNet' while running in aot-only mode because a dependency cannot be found or it is out of date`. Mono emits that to os_log rather than stderr, so it took a `log stream` capture to see at all. Debug neither trims nor AOT-compiles, so **every smoke recorded against this app in the entire project so far was a Debug smoke** and could not have caught it. The `macos` job therefore *runs* the binary and fails on early exit or a native-crash banner; a bundle-shape check would have shipped this.
    - *The fix, and the fix that was rejected:* `<MtouchInterpreter>Docker.DotNet,Newtonsoft.Json</MtouchInterpreter>`, Release + Catalyst only. Both assemblies were present in the bundle, so this is the *out of date* half of the message — full trimming rewrites Newtonsoft.Json and Docker.DotNet's AOT image no longer matches what it was compiled against (a netstandard2.1 assembly with no trim annotations is exactly the shape that breaks this way). The obvious alternative, `TrimMode=partial`, was **rejected for a concrete reason**: it would also stop trimming `Microsoft.ML.OnnxRuntime`, and this project depends on full trimming there to remove the unused `RegisterCustomOps` P/Invoke — so it would have swapped a startup crash for the native link failure the ONNX comments in the csproj describe. Debug re-verified afterwards: 0 errors, the Debug-only ONNX stub target still intact.
    - *No signing identity was used, and none is needed.* macOS ships ad-hoc-signed — what the toolchain does by default — and the DMG carries `INSTALL.txt` covering the quarantine flag, since unsigned bundles hit *"is damaged and can't be opened"* which right-click→Open does **not** reliably bypass on current macOS. Windows ships **unpackaged self-contained, zipped, deliberately not MSIX**: an unsigned MSIX cannot be installed by double-click unless the user first trusts a certificate, whereas an unpackaged folder simply runs. Both `INSTALL.txt` files state the real cost of being unsigned rather than burying it — on macOS that is REQ-FN-039's memory-only secret store.
    - *Signing is gated so it cannot be faked:* each platform job maps its certificate secret into job env and, if the secret is **present**, fails with an explicit error saying 038c is not implemented. The failure mode is a red build, never an ad-hoc artefact published under a name that implies it was signed. The env-mapping trick is the same one `publish-nuget.yml` uses because Actions forbids `secrets` in a job-level `if`.
    - *Two incidental findings:* (1) `nuget.config` is gitignored (it holds the REQ-NFR-001 PAT), so CI has no TrBlazeUI feed — resolved by adding the source from `GITHUB_TOKEN` + `github.repository_owner`, which means **the PAT never has to enter CI** and REQ-NFR-001's rotation is not a blocker for this pipeline. (2) The `test` job needs no feed credentials at all, because `TechieDesk.Tests` references only `TechieDesk.Core` and Core carries no TrBlazeUI or MAUI dependency — an unplanned dividend of the REQ-FN-035 Core extraction, which is what lets a cheap ubuntu runner gate the expensive ones.
    - ⚠ **NOT EXECUTED — the `windows` job has never run, and neither has the workflow as a whole.** Running a GitHub Actions workflow requires pushing it, which is manual in this project; and no Windows machine exists here. The Windows head has still never been compiled by anything (REQ-FN-035 remains PARTIAL on that basis and this workflow does **not** close it — it only creates the first mechanism that *could*). Expect the Windows job to need iteration on its first real run. Its startup smoke is deliberately `continue-on-error` and labelled advisory, because a WinUI app on a headless runner may fail for display reasons unrelated to the build and a gate I have never seen execute should not be able to produce false red builds; the macOS smoke is a hard gate precisely because it was validated against a real crash. **The YAML is syntax-valid (parsed, 5 jobs, dependency graph checked) — that is not the same as proven, and no part of this row should be recorded as a runtime pass on CI.**
    - ⚠ *Found in passing, out of scope, not fixed:* the Release restore surfaces **NU1904 — a known CRITICAL severity advisory** in `Microsoft.AspNetCore.DataProtection` 10.0.0 (GHSA-9mv3-2cwr-p262), plus six NU1903 high-severity advisories in `System.Security.Cryptography.Xml` 10.0.0, both via `TechieDesk.Core`. The existing REQ-NFR-004 notes track NU1902/HtmlSanitizer only, so this is a distinct and more severe finding. Logged as a blocker; it should be triaged before any packaged build is distributed.

<a id="d-req-fn-039"></a>
- **REQ-FN-039** — AppManager tokens and provider secrets live in the **OS credential store** (BRD-132, added 2026-07-26).
  - *Acceptance:* JWT + refresh tokens and provider API keys are stored via Keychain (macOS) / Windows Credential Manager or DPAPI; nothing sensitive is readable from a plain file or from the WebView; a restart restores the session without re-entry.
  - *Supersedes:* the REQ-FN-032 cookie/handle design and, for the desktop head, the DataProtection key-ring file added under REQ-NFR-004b. The encryption-at-rest property must strengthen, never regress — the OS store binds to the machine and user account, which the file-backed key ring did not.
  - **2026-07-26 [REQ-FN-039] — Implemented (75%), self-smoked.**
    - *Files:* `apps/TechieDesk.Core/Services/Auth/ISecretStore.cs` (new), `EphemeralSecretStore.cs` (new), `PersistedSession.cs` (new), `SignInOutcome.cs` (new), `IDesktopSignInService.cs` (new), `DesktopSignInService.cs` (new), `SessionStore.cs`, `ISessionStore.cs`, `SessionTokenStore.cs`, `DesktopSessionContext.cs`, `DesktopAuthExtensions.cs`, `AuthScreenCodes.cs`; `apps/TechieDesk.Core/Services/TechieRagConfigProtector.cs`, `TechieRagConfigService.cs`, `TechieRagManager.cs`; `apps/TechieDesk/Services/Auth/OsCredentialStore.cs` (new), `apps/TechieDesk/MauiProgram.cs`, `Components/Pages/Auth/Login.razor`, `Components/Pages/Auth/Register.razor`.
    - *Tests:* `tests/TechieDesk.Tests/Auth/SecretStoreSessionTests.cs` (16, new), `tests/TechieDesk.Tests/ProviderSecretStoreTests.cs` (6, new); harness updates in `Auth/SessionTestHarness.cs`, `Support/LicensingFakes.cs`; 2 rewrites in `Auth/SessionContinuityTests.cs`.
    - *What is now stored where:* AppManager access + refresh tokens, the signed-in identity, the captured licence and both session deadlines are serialized to ONE OS-store entry, `techiedesk.session.v1`. Provider API keys go to `techiedesk.provider.<field>`. `techierag-config.json` holds `enc:v2:<field>` references — a field NAME, never key material. Nothing token-shaped is written to any file, handed to the WebView, or placed in a cookie (there is no cookie left).
    - *What the DataProtection key ring is now for:* reading `enc:v1:` values an existing install already has on disk, and protecting keys on a host with no durable credential store. It is legacy/fallback, no longer the protection of record — so the REQ-NFR-004b property strengthens rather than regressing.
    - ⚠ *Platform gap, unresolved and NOT code-fixable here:* Mac Catalyst refuses `SecureStorage` to a build without a provisioning-profile-backed `keychain-access-groups` entitlement (`MissingEntitlement` / -34018 — Apple's documented Catalyst requirement, not a MAUI defect). Adding the entitlement makes the app **fail to launch** under an ad-hoc signature (`Launchd job spawn failed`; build warning MT7139), so it was reverted. This lands on **REQ-FN-038**, which owns the signing identity: once the app is signed, `OsCredentialStore` needs no change — its startup probe simply starts succeeding. Until then the head runs memory-only for secrets and says so in the boot log.
    - ⚠ *Open:* the sliding idle window is not applied to a session restored after a restart (hard deadline only) — deliberate, documented in `SessionStore.RestorePersistedSession`, and reversible without a stored-format change since the idle deadline is already in the payload.

<a id="d-req-fn-040"></a>
- **REQ-FN-040** — `DockerContainerService` drives a **configurable** Docker daemon (BRD-134, added 2026-07-26 per owner decision).
  - *Acceptance:* the daemon endpoint is a setting — local socket, LAN address, or remote TCP/TLS — and the full Qdrant lifecycle (status/start/stop/restart/pull/logs) works against whichever is configured; the active endpoint is visible in the UI and a connection test reports failures honestly.
  - *Explicitly retained:* F-QADMIN was NOT retired by the desktop pivot. What the pivot removed is containerizing *TechieDesk*; administering a *Qdrant* container remains a differentiator, and is now more useful because the daemon need not be local.
  - ⚠ *Security:* a Docker daemon endpoint is effectively root on the target host. Endpoint credentials/client certs belong in the OS credential store (REQ-FN-039), TLS verification must be on by default, and configuring a plain unauthenticated TCP endpoint should warn.
  - **2026-07-26 [REQ-FN-040] / [REQ-UI-042] — Implemented (75%), self-smoked.** Files: `apps/TechieDesk/Services/DockerDaemonEndpoint.cs` (new), `apps/TechieDesk/Services/DockerDaemonSettings.cs` (new), `apps/TechieDesk/Services/DockerContainerService.cs`, `apps/TechieDesk/Components/Pages/QdrantAdmin.razor`. Tests: `tests/TechieDesk.Tests/Docker/` (31, all green; proven RED by four behaviour-removal mutations). Smoke: `tests/verify/req-fn-040-req-ui-042-docker-daemon.spec.ts`, 4/4 at 1280 and 430.
  - ⚠ **Lifecycle operations exercised against a real Docker daemon: NONE** — this host has no daemon running. `status/start/stop/restart/pull/logs/create/remove` are unit- and compile-verified only. What was driven live is the configuration + connection-test + honest-failure path.
  - ⚠ *Open:* `IDockerDaemonSettingsStore` is composed inside `DockerContainerService` rather than DI-registered, because `Program.cs` was owned by another agent this wave — a one-line `AddSingleton` would let it be injected properly. The client-certificate path/password are read from the `Docker:` configuration section as the marked REQ-FN-039 seam; no second secret store was introduced.

<a id="d-req-fn-041"></a>
- **REQ-FN-041** — Delete the code retired by the desktop-only amendment (added 2026-07-26).
  - *Scope:* role/capability/authz stack (`ProductRoleMapper`, `CapabilityService`, `IAuthGuard`/`AuthGuard`, `CapabilityDeniedException`, user↔workspace assignment) — REQ-FN-005/006/007; `Dockerfile`, `docker-compose.yml`, `.env.example` — REQ-FN-017/018/019; the cookie/session machinery (`SessionCookie`, `SessionHandle`, `SessionStore`, `SessionStoreOptions`, `ISessionStore`, `ISessionContext`, `SessionContext`, `SessionEndpoints`, `POST /auth/login`) — REQ-FN-032; the Postgres 0001 migration script set — REQ-FN-031; and the tests covering each.
  - *Acceptance:* deleted, not commented out or left unreferenced; the solution builds and the remaining test suite passes; no orphaned DI registrations.
  - ⚠ *Sequencing:* must run AFTER REQ-FN-035/036/039. Removing the session machinery before the MAUI head and the credential store exist would break the working Blazor head with nothing to replace it.
  - ✅ *2026-07-26 status:* role/capability/authz stack + user↔workspace assignment DELETED (second pass); Docker/compose/env, Postgres scripts and the ASP.NET auth files deleted in the first pass. **Still standing, on purpose:** `SessionCookie`, `SessionHandle`, `SessionStore`, `SessionStoreOptions`, `ISessionStore`, `ISessionContext` — REQ-FN-039 rebuilt the desktop session on top of them (expiry, handle rotation, all-devices logout) and `SessionCookie.HandleClaimType` is the claim `TechieDeskAuthenticationStateProvider` reads, so the REQ-FN-032 line of this scope is superseded rather than outstanding. The unused `WorkspaceAssignment` **database table** is also left in place — dropping a journaled DbUp table against a user's real data is its own decision, tracked separately.

<a id="d-req-fn-042"></a>
- **REQ-FN-042** — Background scheduler service (BRD-139, added 2026-07-26).
  - *Acceptance:* a single toggle installs/removes a per-user helper (macOS launchd user agent, Windows per-user service); with it on, a schedule fires with the main window closed; with it off, schedules run only while the app is open and a missed run catches up at next launch. Run conditions (mains power, named networks, wake-for-run) are honoured.
  - *Design constraint:* the helper hosts the **same in-process scheduler** as the app. No separate server process, no job database, no listening port, no third-party job framework — otherwise the desktop pivot's simplicity is given straight back.
  - ⚠ *Platform reality:* installing a login item is a user-visible OS-level action (macOS will prompt; Windows may require elevation depending on the mechanism). The toggle must state what it installs and where, and uninstall must actually remove it.

<a id="d-req-fn-054"></a>
- **REQ-FN-054** — Own repository; the three library packages consumed from NuGet (BRD-146, added 2026-09-03). Plan: `docs/TechieRag.Agents-Proposal.md` §10.
  - *Acceptance:* (1) the TechieDesk repository holds `apps/*` (five projects), `tests/TechieDesk.Tests`, `tests/appium`, `tests/verify` + `playwright.config.ts`, `.github/workflows/publish-desktop.yml`, the TechieDesk docs, mockups, screenshots, `uiIssues/`, and an app-configured `.tfcore/` (`metrics.project_type: app`, `runtimeVerification` block); (2) `TechieDesk.Core.csproj` references `TechieRag` and `TechieRag.Embedded` (and, for REQ-RAG-053, `TechieRag.Agents`) as `PackageReference`s; no project in the repository has a `ProjectReference` to a library project; (3) `Directory.Packages.props` pins every package version centrally; `NuGet.config` lists nuget.org, GitHub Packages and a local folder feed, and no committed pin carries a `-local` suffix; (4) a new `TechieDesk.slnx` builds 0 errors and `TechieDesk.Tests` passes from packages alone; (5) the Catalyst head launches from the package build and the screen sweep runs from this repository; (6) `PROJECT-STATUS.md` and `DECISIONS.md` here carry the app entries with a cross-link to the library repository.
  - ⚠ *Owner-run half:* creating the repository and moving history is git work the harness never performs; the agent half is items 2–6 once the repository exists.
  - *Ordering:* the library repository deletes `apps/` (TechieRag `REQ-FN-006`) only after item 4 here is green.

<a id="d-req-rag-053"></a>
- **REQ-RAG-053** — The agent runtime is `TechieRag.Agents` on Microsoft Agent Framework (BRD-147, added 2026-09-03). Design: `docs/TechieRag.Agents-Proposal.md` §5.
  - *Acceptance:* (1) `WorkspaceChat` and `WorkspaceFlowService` compose the permitted `IToolHandler` (skills ∩ catalogue via `AgentToolPlanner`, `EgressGate` wrapping, per-turn MCP tools) exactly as today and hand it, a `DelegateRetrievalSource` over `WorkspaceManager.SearchScopedAsync` with the turn's scope, `agent.MaxToolCalls`, the `TimeLimitSeconds` token, the agent's instructions and the `AgentTrace` progress sink to `TechieRagAgentBuilder` (`UseConfiguredLlm()` — the workspace/agent model through the library's `ILlmProvider` adapter); no app code calls `AgentLoopRunner`; (2) the `rag-search` skill binds to the library's `KnowledgeBaseTools` and returns the structured result (refs, scores, status, hint); `list_documents` is offered under the `rag-search` catalogue permission; `RetrievalTurnState.Collected` feeds `ToCitations`; (3) `AgentSystemPrompt` composes `AgenticInstructions.Default` + the agent's instructions or workspace prompt as domain guidance + a one-line relaxation when `AllowGeneralKnowledge` is on; (4) flows run an agent node through the library's `AIToolHandler` adapter and `HostGuardrails` still see every tool call; scheduled agent runs need only the package reference; (5) `ConfirmEgress` still suspends the gated call and a decline still reports `SkillUnavailable`; HTTP MCP tools stay gated and stdio tools ungated; MCP server provenance still resolves; (6) the trace renders the same four step kinds and `FailureMessage` codes localize as before; (7) every existing TechieDesk.Tests agent, flow, egress and MCP suite passes unchanged against the new runtime; (8) live smoke on the running head: an `@agent` turn with a real tool call, an egress prompt answered both ways, citations and a rendered trace.
  - *Dependency:* `TechieRag.Agents` ≥ 1.0.0-preview carrying TechieRag `REQ-RAG-015/016/017`.
  - ⚠ *Guard:* the old `AgentLoopRunner` path is removed from the app only after item 7 is green.

<a id="d-req-rag-047"></a>
- **REQ-RAG-047** — LIB: per-workspace rerank switch (new 2026-07-20, TR-RAG-005).
  - *Problem:* `Workspace.RerankEnabled` persists and round-trips but nothing reads it — `ITechieRag.SearchAsync` exposes no per-call rerank switch, so reranking is global configuration only and the per-workspace toggle is inert.
  - *Acceptance:* enabling/disabling rerank on one workspace changes that workspace's retrieval ordering and leaves other workspaces unaffected; asserted by a test that fails if the flag is ignored. Restores REQ-RAG-014 to a verifiable state.
<a id="d-req-rag-048"></a>
- **REQ-RAG-048** — LIB: signal context truncation (new 2026-07-20, TR-RAG-006).
  - *Problem:* `PromptConfig.MaxContextChunks` (default 5) silently truncates the merged pinned+retrieved context. Pinned chunks survive only as a side effect of ordering; more than 5 pinned documents evict every retrieved result with no signal.
  - *Acceptance:* callers can detect that truncation occurred (event/flag/log) and pinned-vs-retrieved eviction is deliberate rather than incidental.

<a id="d-req-rag-049"></a>
- **REQ-RAG-049** — Email / IMAP connector (BRD-135, added 2026-07-26 on owner review).
  - *Acceptance:* a mailbox can be connected, scoped (folders, from-date, sender, subject), and ingested into a workspace; messages and selected attachment types become searchable and citable; a second run ingests only what is new; every run reports per-message results and failure reasons like any other connector (BRD-65).
  - *Security:* IMAP credentials/tokens go to the OS credential store (REQ-FN-039). TLS is mandatory — a plaintext connection is refused, not warned about.
  - ⚠ *Privacy:* everything ingested becomes answerable in that workspace. The scope filters and the "include messages you sent" / "include spam" defaults (both off) are acceptance criteria, not preferences.
  - *Library boundary:* the IMAP fetch + message parsing belong in the TechieRag library as a connector alongside GAP-LIB-05/06, not in app code — TechieDesk surfaces only the configuration UI and the job view.

<a id="d-req-rag-052"></a>
- **REQ-RAG-052** — `EmbeddedEmbeddingProvider` must encode text the way XLM-RoBERTa expects: SentencePiece piece ids shifted by the fairseq offset (spm `0` → `<unk>` = 3) and wrapped in `<s>` … `</s>`, matching `OnnxCrossEncoderReranker.ToModelId` (TR-RAG-044, BRD-116/BRD-8, added 2026-08-03).
  - *Acceptance:* the cross-lingual diagnostic passes — a Hindi query scores a relevant English passage above an irrelevant one, at a similarity consistent with a real BGE-M3 match rather than the ~0.35 noise floor measured today; same-language retrieval does not regress; stored vectors carry a version stamp so a corpus embedded by the old code is DETECTED rather than silently mixed with new ones; the required re-ingestion is documented for users.
  - ⚠ *Blocked on an owner decision, not on work.* The fix changes every vector the provider produces, so every embedding already ingested becomes incompatible and the corpus must be re-indexed. Better retrieval against a full re-ingest is the owner's trade to make.

- **REQ-NFR-003** — Performance: <2s streaming overhead; <200ms UI interactions; 10-page PDF embedded <60s (local BGE-M3); ≥25 concurrent chat users (BRD-94).
<a id="d-req-nfr-004"></a>
- **REQ-NFR-004** — Security: TLS to AppManager/providers; tokens server-side only; server-side authz; OWASP input hygiene; API keys hashed (BRD-95).
<a id="d-req-nfr-005"></a>
- **REQ-NFR-005** — Accessibility: keyboard nav, focus states, ARIA, WCAG 2.1 AA contrast (BRD-96).
<a id="d-req-nfr-006"></a>
- **REQ-NFR-006** — Every screen usable @1280 and @390 with `scrollWidth == viewport` (BRD-97). Playwright-gated.
<a id="d-req-nfr-007"></a>
- **REQ-NFR-007** — Evergreen Chrome/Edge/Firefox/Safari (BRD-98).
<a id="d-req-nfr-008"></a>
- **REQ-NFR-008** — Data locality: nothing leaves the instance except LLM/embedding-provider and AppManager calls; no telemetry (BRD-99).
<a id="d-req-nfr-009"></a>
- **REQ-NFR-009** — Serilog rolling-file logging under `logs/` in every executable head (app, TechieDeskDb, future heads); unhandled exceptions logged (BRD-100).
<a id="d-req-nfr-010"></a>
- **REQ-NFR-010** — Resilience: AppManager outage → grace (REQ-FN-015); provider failures surface library retry/fallback with visible status; restart loses no persisted data (BRD-101).
<a id="d-req-nfr-011"></a>
- **REQ-NFR-011** — Runtime UI verification harness for the MAUI head (added 2026-07-26). **This is workflow infrastructure, not a product feature**, and it is a hard dependency of every future `*verify` of the desktop head.
  - *Why:* the smoke, visual-truth, and verifier gates that produced most of this checklist's runtime evidence drive a browser over `localhost` with Playwright. A MAUI Blazor Hybrid app has no URL to navigate to and cannot be driven that way.
  - *Acceptance:* the `runtimeVerification` block in `.tfcore/core-config.yaml` is live — Appium `mac2` for Mac Catalyst, FlaUI or Appium-Windows for Windows — and the verifier can capture screenshots plus an element tree from a running desktop build and feed both into the existing data-render (§4a) and visual-truth (§4b) gates.
  - ⚠ *Until it exists:* the verifier degrades every UI requirement to `⚠ STATIC-ONLY`. A static pass must never be recorded as a runtime pass.
  - ⚠ *Re-verification debt:* the UI rows currently marked `Verified` were proven against the Blazor Server head under Playwright. They are not evidence for the MAUI head and must not be carried over as passes once the head changes.

| TR-RAG-003 no workspace-scoped streaming-with-sources | TechieRag | Minor | **Fixed (pending verify)** | 2026-07-20 FIXED — WorkspaceManager.AskStreamWithSourcesAsync(workspaceId, question, history?, options?, ct) emits RagStreamEvent in the REQ-RAG-024 order Sources→Token(s)→Completed, scoped to the workspace doc set, honoring per-ws topK/threshold/system-prompt/model/chat-vs-query, and merging pinned docs ahead of retrieved results. | TR-RAG-005 per-workspace rerank not honored | TechieRag | Major | **Fixed (pending verify)** | 2026-07-20: Workspace.RerankEnabled is dead config — no per-call rerank switch on ITechieRag.SearchAsync. Caused REQ-RAG-014 to be demoted from Verified. **2026-07-25 FIXED via REQ-RAG-047** — SearchOptions.Rerank per-call switch + WithRerankEnabledByDefault; the workspace flag is now authoritative in both directions. 5 tests, all verified to go red without the flag read |
| TR-RAG-006 MaxContextChunks silent truncation | TechieRag | Minor | **Fixed (pending verify)** | 2026-07-20: PromptConfig.MaxContextChunks (default 5) truncates merged context with no signal; >5 pinned docs evict all retrieved results silently. **2026-07-25 FIXED via REQ-RAG-048** — explicit eviction policy + WasTruncated/eviction counts + ContextTruncated event + warning log; sources now match what the prompt received. ⚠ the non-workspace path is still silent — split out as TR-RAG-009 |
| TR-RAG-007 Chunk.Metadata is JsonElement, not string | TechieRag | Minor | Open | 2026-07-20: metadata values round-trip from the store as JsonElement, so consumers pattern-matching `value is string` hit a silent fallback — this is exactly why every citation chip rendered a bare GUID instead of the document name (fixed app-side). Library should expose a typed metadata accessor |
| TR-RAG-008 streaming UI needs explicit render yield | TechieRag | Nice-to-have | Open | 2026-07-20: StateHasChanged() after the Sources event is not flushed before the LLM call, so citation chips never paint when the provider fails fast; needed `await Task.Yield()`. Worth documenting for SDK consumers building streaming UIs |
| TR-008 TrBlazeUI a11y defects | TrBlazeUI | **Major** (reduced scope) | Open (upstream) — **CORRECTED 2026-08-01** | 🔴 **FOUR OF THIS ENTRY'S ORIGINAL CLAIMS ARE FALSE and were propagating into build-phase briefs.** Corrected against the shipped `TrBlazeUI.Components.xml` 1.0.7 by reflection, a live axe run on a purpose-built probe app, and Appium/mac2 probing of the running Catalyst head. See `docs/TechieRag-TrBlazeUI-Feedback.md` §"TR-008 — AMENDED" and §"TR-008 — CLARIFICATION". **FALSE:** (1) *"SelectTrigger silently ignores AriaLabel, so 6 controls are UNNAMEABLE"* — `SelectTrigger<T>` **has** `AdditionalAttributes`; a raw lowercase `aria-label` lands on the rendered `<button role="combobox">` and passes axe. The original diagnosis confused the ATTRIBUTE with a PARAMETER: `AriaLabel="x"` is not a parameter there, so Blazor splatted it verbatim as the meaningless `arialabel="x"`. All 9 app comboboxes were fixed this way. (2) *"Progress accepts AriaLabel but never forwards it"* — `Progress` has `AdditionalAttributes`; `aria-label` forwards correctly. (3) *"FieldLabel renders `<label>` with no `for` and no For parameter (98 sites)"* — `FieldLabel` **has** both `For` and `AdditionalAttributes`. (4) 🔴 *"passing AriaLabel THROWS InvalidOperationException at RUNTIME"* — **it does not throw at all.** An `AriaLabel` written against a component that does not declare it is splatted as `arialabel="…"`: **no exception, no build warning, no accessible name.** The failure is SILENCE — and describing it as a crash is actively harmful, because it invites builders to believe a green run proves the name was emitted. ⚠ The standing guidance is unchanged and still correct: **write the raw lowercase `aria-label` attribute, never an `AriaLabel` parameter, on components that do not declare one.** ✅ **STILL CONFIRMED OPEN:** `Slider` (no `AriaLabel`, no `AdditionalAttributes`) and `FileUpload` (no splat; its `<input type=file>` gets a random GUID id, and wrapping it in a `<label>` does not satisfy axe) are **genuinely unnameable**; `CardTitle` hardcodes `<h3>` and `AlertTitle` `<h5>` with no splat, so a correct document outline is impossible (the whole of the 25 remaining `heading-order` nodes); `LucideIcon` emits `role="img"` unconditionally with no name; Pagination `ul`/`li` malformed + unnamed icon buttons; sidebar focus rings depend on Tailwind JIT utilities the prebuilt bundle never emits. ⚠ *"Tabs aria-controls points at unmounted panels (8 critical)"* also drove the REQ-UI-037/038/039 demotions and was a **harness artefact**: WebKit maps `role="tab"` to `XCUIElementTypeTab`, a type the 2026-07-28 probe never queried — the tab opens fine and both panels render. ~~Original 2026-07-20 text: SelectTrigger AriaLabel never emitted / 6 UNNAMEABLE controls; Slider + FileUpload throw InvalidOperationException at RUNTIME; Progress never forwards; FieldLabel no `for`/`For` (98 sites); Tabs aria-controls unmounted (8 critical).~~ Kept struck through for audit |
| TR-009 TrBlazeUI ships a vulnerable transitive dependency | TrBlazeUI | **Major** | Open (upstream) | 2026-07-20: TrBlazeUI.Components 1.0.7 → HtmlSanitizer 9.0.892 → AngleSharp `[0.17.1]` exact pin, which carries NU1902 / GHSA-pgww-w46g-26qg (moderate mXSS). No STABLE HtmlSanitizer release exists on AngleSharp ≥1.5.0, so consumers have no clean stable path. App worked around it by pinning HtmlSanitizer 9.1.949-beta (pulls AngleSharp 1.5.1 + AngleSharp.Css 1.0.0-beta.216 — two PRE-RELEASE packages in a distributable product). Please upgrade the HtmlSanitizer dependency |
| TR-010 DropdownMenuTrigger emitted twice into the DOM | TrBlazeUI | Minor | Open (upstream) | 2026-07-20: trigger button appears twice (in-grid + portaled duplicate) — breaks getByRole counting in tests and is an a11y concern (duplicate interactive controls with identical accessible names) |
| TR-RAG-004 workspace doc API gaps | TechieRag | Minor | Open | 2026-07-18 (Wave 4): IWorkspaceStore.RemoveDocumentAsync deletes only the membership row (not vectors, not other ws) despite contract; WorkspaceDocument is a thin membership record (no name/type/size/chunkCount/uploadDate → app joins global ListDocumentsAsync, no size field anywhere); IngestFileAsync takes a path not a Stream; dedupe is silent (no reused flag). App worked around all four. Suggest richer WorkspaceDocument + delete-vectors-everywhere API + Stream ingest + dedupe result flag |
| TR-RAG-009 global path still truncates silently | TechieRag | Minor | Open | 2026-07-25: REQ-RAG-048 fixed the WORKSPACE context path, but `PromptTemplateEngine.FormatContext` still does `searchResults.Take(config.MaxContextChunks)` with no flag, event or log — so plain `ITechieRag.AskAsync` / `AskStreamWithSourcesAsync` consumers (not going through WorkspaceManager) still cannot detect truncation. Same fix, other seam |
| TR-RAG-010 pinned-chunk retrieval is O(n) round-trips | TechieRag | Minor | Open | 2026-07-25: `WorkspaceManager.CollectPinnedChunksAsync` issues one vector search PER pinned document — 6 pinned docs produced 7 searches per question (observed live). Fine at this scale, but O(n) round-trips at 50+ pinned docs. Suggest a single batched/filtered query |
| TR-RAG-011 pinned chunks bypass rerank | TechieRag | Nice-to-have | Open | 2026-07-25: pinned-document chunks deliberately do NOT go through the reranker even when the workspace rerank toggle (REQ-RAG-047) is on — kept stable/low-risk in that change. Worth an explicit product decision on whether reranking should apply within pinned documents |
| TR-RAG-012 RerankConfig.Enabled semantics changed | TechieRag | Informational | Documented | 2026-07-25 (REQ-RAG-047): `RerankConfig.Enabled` no longer means "build and use a reranker" — it now means "rerank by DEFAULT for calls that don't specify". The reranker is constructed whenever a usable source + credentials exist. Needs a release-note line for SDK consumers before the next NuGet publish |
| TR-011 Input cannot take part in a plain form post | TrBlazeUI | **Major** | Open (upstream) | 2026-07-25 (hit by REQ-FN-032): `<Input>` exposes no `Name` parameter and no `CaptureUnmatchedValues`, so it cannot participate in an HTML form POST. The session fix requires a real form post (the cookie can only be written during an HTTP request, not from inside an interactive circuit), so /login and /register had to hand-copy Input's emitted class string onto raw `<input>` elements to stay pixel-identical. Same for `<Button>` needing `type="submit"`. Please add a `Name` parameter or attribute splatting |
| TR-012 Select option names match on substring | TrBlazeUI | Minor | Open (upstream) | 2026-07-25: `getByRole('option', {name: 'OpenAI (Cloud)'})` also matches 'Azure OpenAI (Cloud)', so every Playwright spec touching these selects needs `exact: true`. Test-ergonomics only, no product impact |

Harmless but it buries real warnings in build output |

| TR-007 grid-cols utilities missing | TrBlazeUI | Minor | Open (upstream) | 2026-07-18: prebuilt trblazeui.css (1.0.7) ships flex/gap/space-y but NOT grid-cols-*/lg:grid-cols-* → any `grid-cols-N` collapses to 1 col (also affects pre-existing Settings.razor). App workaround: `.td-grid-2`/`.td-grid-2col` in base.css. Ask upstream to ship grid-cols utils or document app-side Tailwind build |
| TR-RAG-013 `ConnectorException` is sealed and English-only | TechieRag | Minor | Open | 2026-08-02 (hit by REQ-UI-056): `TechieRag.Connectors.ConnectorException` is `sealed` and carries only a `string Message`, so an APP-authored run-level refusal cannot be expressed as one and cannot carry a localizable code. The app had to add its own `ConnectorSetupException` and catch **two** types at every call site, reconciling them in `ConnectorSetupException.ReasonFor(ex)`. Unsealing it, or adding a `(code, args)` payload, would remove that duplication. ⚠ Related: `ConnectorItemFailure.Reason` and `ConnectorPage.Failures` carry finished English from the library and are stored verbatim, so a Hindi run history still shows library wording for source-level failures — not fixable app-side |
| TR-RAG-014 `Models.AgentStep` / `Models.ToolResult` have no code carrier | TechieRag | Minor | Open | 2026-08-02 (found by REQ-RAG-050): neither type can carry a message code, so the `ToolExecuted` row an **Agent** node produces for a refused tool call still renders English `ErrorMessage`. `FlowRunner` emits a properly coded `GuardrailBlocked` row alongside it, so the user IS told correctly — but the duplicate row stays untranslatable. Closing it is one additive nullable property on each type; Cluster C stayed inside `Orchestration/` as scoped rather than reach for it |
| TR-RAG-015 `AgentToolHandler.ForFlow` contradicts its own contract | TechieRag | **Major** | Open → `REQ-RAG-051` | 2026-08-02: the XML doc says a blocked or budget-exhausted inner run "comes back as an UNSUCCESSFUL tool result", but the code returns `IsSuccess` defaulting to **true**, so **the trace renders a blocked call GREEN**. Fourth instance of the lying-surface pattern (`REQ-NFR-013`, `REQ-FN-052`, `REQ-FN-010`). Raised as its own REQ because it is a behaviour change, not a localization one |
